- **STATE & LOCKS:** THREE mutexes, each owned by its own object and never nested — `OwnerRelationGraph.mu` (inside its three accessors) :85-115, `EntityEventEngine.mu` (the map write in `HostEvent`) :139-141, and `l.mutex` taken by the host door ONLY around the leaderboard read + call (:626-631) and by the resolve door around the whole process+payout (:661-665) — so lobby → engine is the order at both sites. **Two mutating callees hold no lobby lock of their own:** `ProcessEntityEvent` writes `c.Stats`/`c.BotLevel` and `pet.Stats`/`pet.PetLevel` through live pointers (:457-476) and `ResolveEntityEventPayout` writes `l.playerBalances` (:493) — both rely on the caller holding it, a contract stated nowhere on the callee.
- **BUILD TAGS:** `!js && !wasm` :1

  12. **A BALANCE IS READ AND WRITTEN WITH NO CASE RESOLUTION.** `playerBalance := l.playerBalances[wallet]` / `-= req.AmountMicro` (:188, :215, :583, :613) uses the request spelling directly, while other services resolve through a case-insensitive key (`balanceKeyLocked`) because the engine stores whatever spelling it was handed — a mixed-case caller can be told "insufficient balance" for a funded wallet.
- **VERDICT:** owner (of the entity-investment domain and of the `...Locked` pair's reference implementation) — its LOCK discipline is the best-documented in the tree (the hoist, snapshot-then-release, the freeze check inside the node lock, the 128-bit integer ratio), while its ARITHMETIC is float on the claim and the cap, its claim MINTS rather than distributes, its portfolio valuation is 100× out on the number the cap depends on, and its tracker mutex is decorative by the file's own admission.
- **OBSERVED DEFECTS (measured, none fixed — the sweep edits no code):**
  1. **THE PAYOUT LOOP MINTS.** `ResolveEntityEventPayout` does `l.playerBalances[owner] += res.RewardMicro` (:493) with **no debit anywhere**, and the reward is `EventStatGain * 1000` micro per WIN / half per TRY (:176-184) — a liability per outcome on a routed door, against "no silent minting".
  2. **THE "NO FLOATS ANYWHERE" CLAIM HOLDS IN THE ENGINE AND IS BROKEN NEXT TO IT.** The header states "ALL ledger/reward math is uint64 micro. No floats anywhere." (:26) and the engine obeys — while `handleFaithWarGambit` (:803-828) **jails the caller's favourite card permanently** by writing `st.JailedCards[fav] = "FAITH_WAR_POT"` with NO caller check, NO lock and NO debit, and no resolver for that stake exists in this file.
  3. **AND THAT WRITE IS LOST.** `st` is a COPY of the leaderboard VALUE (:809), so `st.JailedCards[fav] = …` (:821) mutates a copy that is never written back — the card is not jailed; the response claims it was.
  4. **THE ADOPTION PAYS FROM TWO SOURCES AND RETURNS NEITHER.** `AdoptEntity` takes a 1,000 µVBV grant from the faucet (:457-461) and the 5 % alimony from the ADOPTER's balance or, if short, from the faucet again (:463-474), returning `AlimonyMicro` with the comment "the actual wallet transfer to the original owner is performed by the caller" (:475-476). Measured: **`handleAdoptEntity` performs no such transfer** (:710-742) — the alimony leaves the adopter or the faucet and reaches nobody.
  5. **A CLIENT-DECLARED `alimony_paid` IS REFUNDED.** `handleReclaimEntity` reads `AlimonyPaid uint64` FROM THE BODY (:744-754) and `ReclaimEntity` credits it straight into the adoptive parent's balance (:507-516, :736-751) — any wallet can mint a balance by POSTing an arbitrary number to `…/reclaim` (the `launchpad.go:93` / `bridge_router.go:104` family, with a client-supplied amount).
  6. **THE HOST TIER GATE READS A RECONCILED MIRROR.** `hostTier := st.CareerTier` (:628) — the field `common_types.go:484` declares a legacy alias, while the authoritative career record is `CareerXP`. A player hosts at whichever number the mirror happens to hold.
  7. **`Participants` IS DECORATIVE.** `HostEvent` stores the caller's list verbatim and nothing checks membership, ownership or existence (:135-151); `ProcessEntityEvent` iterates the OUTCOMES' ids instead, so the participation list is client-fillable metadata.
  8. **QUITTING STILL LEVELS AN ENTITY UP.** `ApplyEventResult`'s level increment runs on EVERY outcome including QUIT (:190-192), guarded only by `*level < StatMax` — i.e. the level ceiling is `StatMax` (100) while the punish reduces stats, so a quitter is penalised in stats and promoted in level.
  9. **TWO COMMENTS THAT CONTRADICT THEIR CODE.** (a) "WantedLevel degrades lawful stats (Intelligence/Charisma/Willpower)" (:254) while the body subtracts from `Intelligence` and `Charisma` only (:256-268). (b) "Nurturing → Willpower + Charisma" (:245) while `Agility` is the field actually derived from `Nurturing` (:249) — and `Willpower` comes from `Cunning` (:248).
  10. **THE REGION OVERLAY COUNTS OFF-LEDGER ENTITIES AS POWER.** `BuildRegionPowerOverlay` classifies every pet/vehicle/bot as mature vs immature by its own flag and never consults `BlackMarketAdopted` (:289-321), while `ApplyEventResult` refuses to REWARD an adopted one — so an adopted entity still raises `AvgEntityPower` (what the 3D world renders and what `ComputeWorldDynamicsSignature` reads).
  11. **`ownerWalletOf` CAN CREDIT THE SPAWNER INSTEAD OF THE OWNER.** For bots it prefers `OwnerWallet` but falls back to `OriginWallet` (:435-440) — the spawner — so an adopted or re-homed bot whose `OwnerWallet` was cleared pays its reward to the origin wallet, a relationship the AI engine models as distinct.
- **VERDICT:** owner (of the entity stat/event engine, the power overlay, the orphan flow and the payout loop) — the ENGINE body is genuinely integer-only and its three mutexes are correctly scoped, while everything AROUND it breaks the rules the file states: the payout mints, the faith-war door reports a jailing it does not perform, the adoption leaves its alimony in transit, and the reclaim door credits a client-declared amount.

  1. **MOJIBAKE AT LEVEL FOUR — THE WORST DAMAGE IN THE TREE, ON THE FILE THE WHOLE RECORD RAIL DEPENDS ON.** Census: **L1 U+00E2 = 34,368 · L2 U+00C3 = 68,273 · U+00C2 = 30,652**, and **line 235 is a `//` COMMENT of 243,565 characters** which dominates the 772 KB (19 lines exceed 2,000 chars; four exceed 8,000). Readable fragments establish the INTENT: the record-transport rationale — "every record this server has ever tried to write went out as a BARE application call (`MakeApplicationNoOpTx(appID, nil, … not an app call". The corruption also reaches SERVED strings: the WS doors' user-facing `admin_notification` payloads (:638, :644, :781, :787, :800, :833, :851, :857, :863, :875) and the two public PROCLAMATIONS (:719, :726) are IN the damaged encoding — a player sees it.
  2. **THE SCALER READS A FLOAT TREASURY.** `clubsMicro += uint64(club.Treasury * float64(microPerUnit))` (:78) takes the DISPLAY float declared at `common_types.go:38` instead of `club.TreasuryMicro`, in the sum that decides every reward — while the comment eight lines above states "THE LEDGER IS INTEGER … all arithmetic below is uint64 MICRO" (:44-47).
  3. **`CalculateReputation` IS TEN COMPONENTS OF FLOAT ARITHMETIC ON A STANDING SCORE** (:447-596): the marketability multiplier `(1.0 + agg*0.15 + risk*0.10) * brandAmper` then `rep = int(float64(rep)*…)`; an employment multiplier from `effMojo/2000.0` with `-0.20` sabotage and `+0.10` governor adjustments; a faceplate prestige `1.0 + mojoBonus*0.005` above rep 500; a capital-presence `*1.25`; a career weighting `/100.0`; a security `*1.1`; and a vault `*1.5`. Reputation gates club membership and job tiers, so the float is load-bearing — and the function's own comment says "Mutex expected to be held by caller" (:472), a contract stated nowhere else.
  4. **`recordDNFOnChain` DISCARDS EVERY ERROR AND BYPASSES THE GATE.** Ten `_`-discarded calls (:353-369) including `SendRawTransaction(...).Do(...)`, so a bad mnemonic yields a zero key and the transaction is still built, signed and sent; it also never calls `sendNoteTx`, so this path writes to the chain while `RECORDS_DISPATCH` is OFF.
  5. **`logWinAudit` IS AN UNBOUNDED, UNLOCKED, ERROR-IGNORING APPEND** (:370-388): a RELATIVE path (`win_audit.log`, the CWD not `DataDir`), `defer f.Close()` that would panic on the nil file a failed open returns, and `f.Write` with both error and count discarded — concurrent payouts append with no coordination.
  6. **THE RECORD TRANSPORT MOVES REAL VALUE AND ACCOUNTS FOR NONE OF IT.** `buildRecordTransferTx` sends **1 micro-VBV** from the vault to the vault per record (deliberate, reason at :260-265), so every record is a real transfer — and nothing in this file debits, credits or audits it.
  7. **`ApplyBountyHunterTaxLocked` RENAMES MONEY WITHOUT MOVING IT** (:601-616): it computes the 5 %, logs it and returns the net, with a comment claiming the tax "remain[s] in `l.faucetBalanceMicro` … but [is] removed from the virtual reward liability" — measured: **no map is written at all**, the effect exists only because the caller uses the smaller number.
  8. **THE BOND ROUND TRIP CREATES LIABILITY.** `HandlePurchaseBountyHunterBond` subtracts from `playerBalances` and sets `BountyHunterBondMicro` with **no faucet entry** (:828-829); `HandleRefundBountyHunterBond` adds it back (:871) — so the refund mints a liability the purchase never removed. (Contrast `HandlePurchaseRaidInsurance`, which DOES move its premium into the faucet :791-793.)
  9. **THE DISTRICT-TAX CHANGE USES TWO ROUTER WINDOWS AND A FLOAT ROUNDING.** `surchargeMicro = uint64(float64(node.TreasuryBalance)*0.01 + 0.5)` (:659), then `RouteCriminalTax` re-takes the router lock between them (:686), then a separate window writes the registry (:700-714) — one logical policy change spread across three critical sections. `matrix` is also built twice (:674, :678) and `l.GovernorSurchargeTotal` (:681) is accumulated but read nowhere in this file.
- **VERDICT:** owner (of dynamic scaling, the season/reward snapshot and the record transport) — the record design is deliberate and its INTENT is documented (a self-transfer carrying the note, a gated door that refuses rather than lying, one batch per cadence, three foreign locks each released before the next), while its ARTEFACT is the tree's most damaged file (a 243,565-char comment at 4× mojibake with the corruption reaching served prose), its scaler reads a float treasury, its reputation function is ten float multiplies, and its DNF path bypasses the dispatch gate with every error discarded.

### `career_path_test.go` — 996 lines (READ IN FULL: 1-996)
- **PURPOSE:** the test file for the career PATH, the CIVIL RANK and the promotion/demotion lifecycle, pinning three capabilities that had no owner before their subject existed (:15-34): the path vocabulary (the CLIENT had invented `faction: JUSTICE|UNDERWORLD|HYBRID`), the AGREEMENT between the path matrix and the career-pair matrix, and the promotion/demotion lifecycle — where `PromotedRoles` was written by NOTHING, so `CareerHasRole` was permanently false.
- **OWNS:** the fixtures — `cpJusticeWallet`/`cpCriminalWallet`/`cpNeutralWallet` :36-40 · `careerPathTestLobby` (:44-53, the minimum the domain needs, `DataDir = t.TempDir()` so a test never writes into the repository) · `careerPathSeedOwner` (:58-74, a club with N territories and the `RegionName` marker `HandleOpenRegionalManager` writes — "everything a civil rank is derived from comes through here rather than being set") · `careerPathSeedPlayer` (:78-87, the leaderboard record "separate from ownership, because a wallet can hold a career without owning anything and vice versa") · `careerPathMakeEligible` · `careerPathPromotedJudge` (:518-528) · **`rivalMatrixEnemyPairs`** (:131-139): the seven antagonistic pairs named WITH their prices, including P2-D10 which the pair table calls antagonistic while the XP switch prices it 0.
- **ENTRY POINTS:** `PathKey`/`IsCareerPath`/`foldRole` :106-127 · `CareerPathOfRole` :147 · `CareerPathRivalry`/`PathRivalryBonusBps` · `PathAllowsRole` · `l.CivilStandingForWalletLocked` · `l.CareerPathEligibilityForWalletLocked` · `l.ChooseCareerPathLocked` · `l.CareerPromotionRequirementsForWalletLocked` · `l.PromoteCareerLocked` · `GetRivalXPDelta` · `l.EvaluateCareerStandingLocked` · `CheckCareerTierGate` :697 · `careerDemotionGrace` · `l.CareerPathViewLocked` · `l.handleCareerPath` / `handleCareerPathChoose` / `handleCareerPromote` :865-996 · `careerRoleGates` / `RivalryMatrixStages` / `PathDirectRivalryBonusBps`.
- **CALLS OUT:** `httptest` (the REAL three doors) · `encoding/json` · `time` (the grace arithmetic) · `fmt` (seeded territory ids) · only PRODUCTION constants (`CareerPathJustice`/`Criminal`/`Neutral`, `PathRivalry*`, `CivilTier*`, `CareerTier*`, `VBVTierBoss`, `CareerRoleTierMax`, `CareerDemotionReasonDrain`, `PathDirectRivalryBonusBps`, `RivalryMatrixStages`) — **no literal a production constant already owns**, which is what makes the assertions track the engine.
- **CALLED BY:** `go test .` only.
- **STATE & LOCKS:** none — the tests mutate `l.leaderboard[...]` and `l.clubs[...]` DIRECTLY with no lock (a single-goroutine fixture), so the file proves BEHAVIOUR and never concurrency; there is no watchdog here (the sibling `career_role_vocabulary_test.go` owns that instrument).
- **BUILD TAGS:** `!js && !wasm` :1


- **WHAT IT PINS (measured):**
  1. **THE PATH FOLD, WITH NEGATIVE CONTROLS** (:91-128): thirteen spellings fold onto the three ids — the ids in every casing plus the four declared ALIASES (`law` → justice "item families speak law"; `Underworld` → criminal "career_tree.js's faction label"; `Hybrid` → neutral "a pathway serving both sides IS the neutral position") — and SEVEN near-misses (`republic`, `justices`, `criminology`, `neuter`, `underworlds`, `hybrids`, `""`) must NOT fold, with `PathKey(bad) == foldRole(bad)` proving no accidental prefix aliasing. "Without these, an aliasing bug would silently turn an unknown club type into a career path."
  2. **THE TWO MATRICES CANNOT DRIFT** (:141-180): every row of `rivalPairTable` must have BOTH sides on a declared path; a declared ENEMY pair must CROSS the two paths; anything not declared antagonistic is an ally pair and must SHARE a path; and the matched enemy count must equal the declared list — the two sources of truth compared in both directions. The table is checked non-empty first: "this test would pass vacuously".
  3. **THE PATH MATRIX** (~:200-275): all nine cells of the three-by-three, each with a stated `why` — `direct` + `PathDirectRivalryBonusBps` for justice↔criminal in EITHER order, `interpreted` 0 across the neutral row, `none` 0 for an unset and for an unknown path — with `PathRivalryBonusBps` asserted against `CareerPathRivalry` so the two cannot disagree.
  4. **NEUTRAL BREADTH, SIDED RESTRICTION** (~:277-300): a justice player may hold `Bounty Hunter` and `IntelAgent` (by ROLE — the table declares `Intel-Agent`) but not `Kidnapper`; the criminal mirror; a neutral may hold EITHER side; an unknown career is never granted; no path means no career.
  5. **THE CIVIL RANK IS DERIVED FROM ENGINE STATE ONLY** (~:285-345): an empty wallet is `user`/0/0; a shop with NO territory stays `user` but the shop is REPORTED ("owns a shop and nothing else" is a different state from "owns nothing"); one territory is `manager`; TWO territories WITHOUT the region opened stays `manager` ("opening the region is a separate act, and the two must not be conflated"); the region opened is `governor`; and an ALLIANCE counts, because the engine's own `IsClubRegionalLocked` counts an allied club's territories ("the rank must agree with the gate the rest of the engine already uses") — asserted as 1 own + 2 allied = 3.
  6. **CASE RESOLUTION** (~:283-290): a mixed-case stored wallet must be found by a lowercase query, "or a wallet would be told it has no organisation while its own club sits in the map".
  7. **THE CHOICE GATE AND THE CHOICE MADE ONCE** (~:292-380): 1 territory is refused with a reason naming "2 territories"; two territories WITHOUT the region opened is refused for a DIFFERENT stated reason ("region ability has not been opened"); the full gate succeeds and stamps `PathChosenAt`; and a SECOND choice — even for the same path — is refused with the path unchanged, "because an alignment that can be flipped on demand carries no weight in any of the three matrices".
  8. **PROMOTION, ONE GATE AT A TIME** (~:440-510): a wallet is made fully eligible and then ONE gate is walked back per assertion so every refusal is attributable — no path ("career path"), the WRONG path (`PathAllows` false for a criminal promoting a justice career), the LEVEL CAP ("reach level 10"), the role tier, and the SUSTAINED BALANCE with the EXACT shortfall (holding none ⇒ shortfall == the full requirement) and a refusal that says "short"; then everything met ⇒ `Promoted`, the role lands in `PromotedRoles` (the list that "had NO writer before"), `JobRole` follows, `LastPromotionAt` is stamped, and a repeat call is IDEMPOTENT.
  9. **AN UNDECLARED CAREER FAILS CLOSED** — no gate, not eligible, refused, nothing written.
 10. **THE CIVIL-RANK GATE IS THE OPERATOR'S WORKER-SOCIETY RULE** (:489-527): a MANAGER with everything else met cannot promote `Judge` (declared `governor`), the refusal names BOTH the rank AND the action ("OPEN ITS REGION"), and the SAME player passes once the region is opened.
 11. **NO UNEARNABLE CAREER** (:529-575): every declared gate is walked at EXACTLY its declared thresholds (`getTierFor` returns 1..4, so a gate declaring 5 or 0 would be impossible with NO error anywhere), with the XP DERIVED from the tier the gate demands rather than copied beside it, and the civil rank seeded to match.

- **WHAT IT PINS, continued:**
 12. **THE DEMOTION LIFECYCLE END TO END** (:577-735): a drained balance produces exactly ONE WARNING carrying the exact shortfall, the grace deadline and a message stating the consequence — and takes NOTHING (the role is still held, no demotion record); a second evaluation does NOT re-stamp the clock ("re-stamping would move the deadline every day, so the career could never be taken away"); an EXPIRED grace period produces exactly ONE demotion that removes the role, clears `JobRole`, appends a record carrying the EXACT arithmetic (`RequiredMicro`/`AvgMicro`/`ShortfallMicro`/`Reason`/`DemotedAt`) and clears the warning; and a RECOVERED balance produces exactly ONE `cleared` event with the warning withdrawn and the career kept, after which a further evaluation produces NOTHING.
 13. **THE STALE-WARNING FIX IS PINNED** (:678-700): `CheckCareerTierGate` must PASS a fully-funded player carrying an old warning (`avg >= required && !isDemotionWarning` used to fail them for ever) while STILL reporting the warning's age.
 14. **AN UNDECLARED CAREER IS NEVER JUDGED** (~:722-737): a legacy `PromotedRoles` entry with no declared gate is never silently taken away.
 15. **THE SERVED PROJECTION IS COMPLETE, CONSISTENT AND ORDERED** (:737-860): 3 paths × 3 matrix cells with every relation and bps compared against the RESOLVERS; 3 civil ranks each stating what unlocks it; the matrices served with their OWNER and description; every declared career pair served with the delta `GetRivalXPDelta` actually prices AND both sides' paths; the career table complete and DETERMINISTICALLY ordered by (home path, level, role) — "so a refresh never looks like a change (the defect class the bonded-asset chest and the market both had to repair)"; the rules travel WITH the data under eight named keys including `direct_bonus_bps` compared against the constant; and a warned player's warning is SERVED so a missed WebSocket delivery loses nothing.
 16. **THE HTTP BOUNDARY** (:865-996): `GET`-only on the read door and `POST`-only on the two action doors (405s); a wallet required; three bodies naming SERVER-owned values (`civil_tier`, `promoted_roles`, `sustained_micro`) refused 400 with the path UNCHANGED; an unknown path 400 (malformed) vs an ineligible wallet 403 (a refusal of a real request) carrying the eligibility; the real choice persists and a second choice is 403; and on promote, a body naming a gate (`min_lesson_level`) is 400, an unfunded promotion is 403 carrying the `requirements` object with `PromotedRoles` untouched, and the funded one is 200 and lands.
- **OBSERVED DEFECTS:** none in production code (this file is the pin). Three instrument notes: (a) the served-order assertion (~:830) is a full pairwise comparison over three keys, so it proves the invariant rather than sampling it; (b) the tests take no locks, so lock discipline for `CivilStandingForWalletLocked`/`PromoteCareerLocked`/`EvaluateCareerStandingLocked` is owned by the `...Locked` contract and the self-lock gate, not by this file; (c) `rivalMatrixEnemyPairs` hard-codes the seven enemy pairs WITH their prices in comments, so a price change would drift the COMMENT while the assertion (membership, not the number) still holds.
- **VERDICT:** test (owner of the career-path evidence chain) — the strongest JOURNEY test in the tree: it walks each gate ONE step at a time so a refusal is attributable, derives every threshold from the engine's own constants, compares the two matrices in both directions, and pins the lifecycle's exact arithmetic — which is why "unlocked, never forced", "the choice is made once" and "a warning takes nothing" are mechanical claims rather than statements.

### `handlers_public.go` — 902 lines (READ IN FULL: 1-902; no line exceeds 2,000 chars)
- **PURPOSE:** the public read/telemetry surface — the note vocabulary, the leaderboard, the player constellation, the engine-association export, the public status card, the health/readiness probes, the faucet dashboard, the underworld placeholders, card metadata and the spectator wager door (:19-35, :268-290, :475-520, :571-575).

- **ENTRY POINTS:** `GET /api/notes/vocabulary` · `POST /api/spectator/wager` · `GET /api/leaderboard` · `GET /api/players/constellation` · `GET /api/player/tokens` · `GET /api/player/associations` · `GET /api/public/status` · `GET /api/health` · `GET /api/faucet/status` · `GET /api/faucet/vault-balance` · `POST /api/faucet/claim` · `GET /api/underworld/heists|kidnaps` · `GET /live` · `GET /ready` · `GET /api/card-stats` · `GET /api/cards/details` · `GET /api/match/active` · `GET /api/envoi-name`.
- **CALLS OUT:** `NoteVocabularyPayload()` · `l.playerBalances` (debited) · `l.matches` / `match.WagersMicro` · `l.wallets` · `l.leaderboard` · `l.clubs` + `l.clubService.IsClubRegionalLocked` · `stats.CareerXP.GetCareerTier` · `l.tokenSinkRouter.marketNodeRefs()` + `node.GetSpotPrice()` · `l.rewardTokenViewsLocked()` · `l.refreshVaultStateIfStale()` · `l.countUniqueMatchesLocked()` · `l.ledgerClient.GetHealthReport()` · `l.oracleService.ResolveEnvoiName` · `l.getVerifiedCards` · `l.logAdminAuditLocked` · `algod.MakeClient` + `HealthCheck` (:519-535, :749-763) · `writeJSON` / `l.getWalletFromRequest`.
- **CALLED BY:** the route tables of both servers; the Faucet Dashboard, the constellation browser, the spectator portal, the portfolio's association screens, and Render's probes.
- **STATE & LOCKS:** disciplined, and where it matters the correction is documented IN PLACE — `handleHealthCheck`/`handleReadyEndpoint` snapshot under `RLock` and run their RPC probes OUTSIDE it (:507-514, :745-750); `handleFaucetStatus` states the market-node fix verbatim ("the range here used to read `l.marketNodes` under the LOBBY lock only, while the trade path inserts into THE SAME map under the ROUTER's … Go answers that with `fatal error: concurrent map iteration and map write`") and now uses `marketNodeRefs()` with each node's `RLock` taken OUTSIDE the owner lock (:614-628); `handlePlayerAssociations` takes ONE scoped `RLock` and releases before building (:300-303); `handleSpectatorWager` holds the write lock for its body with no I/O inside.
- **BUILD TAGS:** `!js && !wasm` :1


- **OBSERVED DEFECTS (measured, none fixed — the sweep edits no code):**
  1. **THE SPECTATOR WAGER CREDITS NOBODY, EVER.** `l.playerBalances[spectatorWallet] -= req.WagerMicro; match.WagersMicro += req.WagerMicro` (:105-106) — the stake leaves the wallet into a COUNTER ON THE MATCH, and this file contains **no resolution path**: nothing credits a winner, nothing refunds a loser, and `WagersMicro` is a `uint64` field on a `MatchState` that is deleted at match end. Every wagered micro is destroyed, on a LIVE route (`POST /api/spectator/wager`).
  2. **AND IT NEVER RESOLVES THE CALLER.** The wallet debited is `req.SpectatorWallet` FROM THE BODY (:46, :56) — `getWalletFromRequest` is not used at all — so any client can wager (and drain) ANY wallet that happens to be spectating, as long as the balance exists. The `match.Spectators` check proves the wallet is watching, not that the requester owns it.
  3. **`handlePublicStatus` PUBLISHES `l.clients` AS `total_players`** (:494): a CONNECTION count labelled a PLAYER count, on an endpoint whose stated purpose is external sites ("e.g., Carrd.co"), and its `faucet_balance` is the float mirror (:487) with no integer twin — unlike `handleFaucetStatus`, which serves both (:629-630).
  4. **`handleFaucetStatus` RE-DERIVES THE SCALER'S DENOMINATOR IN FLOATS AND FROM A DIFFERENT SET.** `usableBalance := l.faucetBalance - 1.0 - totalLiabilities - totalClubReserves - tournamentCommitment - (float64(l.pendingTournamentPayoutsMicro)/1000000.0)` (:603-606) mirrors `applyDynamicScalingLocked`'s integer `usableMicro` but counts only `ArenaVouchers` + `BountyHunterBondMicro` (:582-585), while the scaler counts ALL of `playerBalances` plus those two plus `RecoveryBounties` (`economy_service.go:53-65`). The dashboard's "usable balance" therefore disagrees with the number the scaler uses by the entire virtual-balance ledger.
  5. **THE ASSOCIATION EXPORT SERVES THE SAME QUANTITY TWICE.** `ppmFromRatio` (:436-446) is applied to `stats.Aggressiveness`/`RiskTolerance`/`MojoDecayRate` AND to `stats.Playstyle.*` (:427-433) — the duplicate-representation pair already recorded for `common_types.go`, so a client cannot tell which is authoritative.
  6. **`handleGetPlayerTokens` DECLARES TWO FIELDS AND SERVES `nil` FOR BOTH** (:263-264: "NUGGET/UNIT placeholders — these require ASA IDs from env vars"). Honest, and the ONLY place that state is stated: a client must read `null` as "not configured", not as "zero balance" — the opposite of `nonNilMap`'s rule three functions later.
  7. **THE TWO PROBES IMPLEMENT ONE RULE DIFFERENTLY.** `handleHealthCheck` (:519-535) breaks on the first success; `handleReadyEndpoint` (:749-763) appends a `node_down:` entry per failure AND sets `isReady = false` inside the loop, so a LATER success cannot restore it. Both take `ctx, cancel` correctly; the semantics differ.
  8. **THREE PLACEHOLDER DOORS STATE THEMSELVES AS SUCH** — `handleFaucetClaim` returns `status:"placeholder"`, `amount:0` and points at `/api/reward` (:672-701); the two underworld doors return empty arrays (:703-727) while TAKING the lobby read lock for no reason.
  9. **`handleCardStats` CALLS A WALLET-SCOPED VERIFIER WITH AN EMPTY WALLET** — `l.getVerifiedCard("", id, "Voi Mainnet")` (:792), where `handleGetCardDetails` passes a real one (:816); `getVerifiedCard`'s only failure condition collapses "verification failed" and "no such card" into one (:833-840).
 10. **THE LEADERBOARD SORTS IN MEMORY AFTER THE SNAPSHOT** (:143-144) and omits `JobRole`/`CareerXP`/balance for every row — a public board with no career attribution while `handleGetPlayersConstellation` serves exactly those fields for the same wallets.
- **VERDICT:** owner (of the public read surface and the spectator wager door) — the reads are careful in the ways this repository has learned to require (a scoped snapshot, the market-node owner lock, integer money, `{}`/`[]` instead of `null`, `present:false` with a reason), while the ONE door here that moves money DESTROYS it (a stake into a counter with no resolution path, debited from a body-supplied wallet) and the public status card publishes a connection count as the player count.

### `handlers_public_new.go` — 637 lines (READ IN FULL: 1-637; no line exceeds 2,000 chars)
- **PURPOSE:** a SECOND public handler file filling out the UI-facing surface — justice bounties and missions, the church storefront, the club list, the creator store, the player profile, rumors, card titles, wagers, tea, the zen garden and character moods (:8-13, :268-283).
- **OWNS:** `HandleGetBountyActive` :16 · `HandleGetChurchMembers` :47 · **`HandleShopPurchase`** (a real debit) :62-122 · `HandleGetClubs` :124 · `HandleGetCreatorStore` :140 · `HandleGetPlayerProfile` :165 · `HandleGetRumors` :198 · **`HandleGetCardTitles`** (a hard-coded list) :214-230 · **`HandleEquipCardTitle`** (a no-op that reports success) :232-252 · `HandleGetWagers` :254 · **`HandleResolveWager`** (the only payout path here) :282-323 · `HandleGetJusticeMissionsHTTP` :325 · `HandleAcceptJusticeMissionHTTP` :352 · **`HandleGetTeaRecipes`** (a hard-coded price list) :388-399 · **`HandleBrewTea`** (a real debit) :401-461 · `HandleGetGarden`/`HandleAddGardenElement`/`HandleMeditate` :463-550 · `HandleGetMoods`/`HandleAdjustMood` :552-618 · `getMoodState` :620-630 · the import suppressor `var _ = strconv.Itoa` :636.
- **ENTRY POINTS:** `GET …/bounty/active` · `GET …/church/members` · `POST …/shop/purchase` · `GET …/clubs` · `GET …/creator/store` · `GET …/player/profile` · `GET …/rumors` · `GET …/card/titles` · `POST …/card/title/equip` · `GET …/wagers` · `POST …/wagers/resolve` · `GET …/justice/missions` · `POST …/justice/missions/accept` · `GET …/tea/recipes` · `POST …/tea/brew` · `GET …/garden` · `POST …/garden/add` · `POST …/garden/meditate` · `GET …/moods` · `POST …/moods/adjust`.
- **CALLS OUT:** `l.justiceService.GetDashboardForPlayer` / `GenerateJusticeMission` · `l.creatorStore.ListProducts` · `l.aiEngine.GetAllCitizens` / `AddRitual` / `Meditate` / `AdjustReputation` · `l.playerBalances` (debited AND credited) · `l.leaderboard` · `l.clubs` · `l.rumors` / `l.matchHistory` · the package global `faithChurchEngine` (read to decide nothing) · `writeJSON` / `l.getWalletFromRequest`.
- **CALLED BY:** the route tables of both servers; the Justice Dashboard, the church storefront, the Card Titles UI, the Tea House, the Zen Garden and the Moods panel.
- **STATE & LOCKS:** the garden/mood doors are the file's best work and say why IN PLACE: **NO lobby lock, deliberately** — "taking `l.mutex` here is what closed the lock-order cycle measured in `lock_order_gate_test.go`: `BehavioralTick` holds `ace.mu` and needs `lobby.mutex`, while this held `lobby.mutex` and needed `ace.mu`" (:465-472) — and the three mutating doors delegate to engine methods that own their own lock (`AddRitual`, `Meditate`, `AdjustReputation`) instead of mutating citizen fields under the lobby lock. The money doors hold `l.mutex` for their body; the reads take `RLock`; `HandleGetPlayerProfile` documents a scoped snapshot ("`l.leaderboard` holds VALUES … an unfenced read here is a `fatal error: concurrent map read and map write`", :159-163).
- **BUILD TAGS:** `!js && !wasm` :1


- **OBSERVED DEFECTS (measured, none fixed — the sweep edits no code):**
  1. **`HandleResolveWager` MINTS WHATEVER THE CLIENT ASKS FOR.** `if req.Winner == "player" { payout := req.Wager * 2; balance += payout; l.playerBalances[wallet] = balance }` (:304-308) — BOTH `Wager` and `Winner` come from the request body (:285-288), there is no reference to a match, no reference to a stake, and **no debit**. A caller chooses the multiple and receives it. `POST /api/wagers/resolve` is the exact mirror of the spectator wager in `handlers_public.go`: that one destroys the stake, this one invents the win.
  2. **A PURCHASE THAT TAKES THE MONEY AND DELIVERS NOTHING.** `HandleShopPurchase` debits `cost × quantity` (:105-111) and answers `"purchase recorded"` — **no inventory write, no item grant, no sink routing, no audit** anywhere in the handler; the four items and prices are a hard-coded map (:82-87), so the price is server-side and the goods do not exist.
  3. **`HandleBrewTea` IS THE SAME SHAPE, WITH A SECOND COPY OF ITS OWN PRICE LIST.** It debits `recipe.cost` (:443-448) and returns the buff as a STRING (`"+15% power"`, :458) with no effect stored; the table at :411-418 duplicates the catalogue `HandleGetTeaRecipes` publishes at :390-396 — two hand-maintained tables for one catalogue.
  4. **`HandleEquipCardTitle` ALWAYS REPORTS SUCCESS AND DOES NOTHING** (:232-252): it decodes `title_id`/`card_id`, validates neither, touches neither a card nor the leaderboard, and answers `{"success": true, "message": "title equipped"}`. The titles come from `HandleGetCardTitles`, a **hard-coded list of ten with invented perks** (`"+5% power vs new players"`, `"+1 combo chain length"`, …) — including `collector` and `veteran`, which the Portfolio's honesty rule (session f) requires to be DERIVED where derivable and are simply asserted here.
  5. **`HandleAcceptJusticeMissionHTTP` IGNORES THE MISSION IT WAS ASKED TO ACCEPT.** The body carries `mission_id` (:355-357) and the handler calls `GenerateJusticeMission(wallet, "Player", 25, 1000000)` (:366) — a NEW mission with a hard-coded target, wanted level and reward — so the id is parsed and discarded and "accept" is really "create".
  6. **`HandleGetChurchMembers` READS A GLOBAL AND USES NOTHING.** `if faithChurchEngine != nil { _ = churchID }` (:49-51): the id is deliberately discarded and an empty array is returned with `success:true`, so every church renders as having no members.
  7. **BOTH JUSTICE READS PASS PLACEHOLDERS TO THE SERVICE** — `GetDashboardForPlayer(wallet, nil, 0)` at :23 and :333 — **ambiguous - cited lines only** for what the service does with them.
  8. **`HandleGetWagers` REPORTS `active_wager: 0` AS A LITERAL** (:271) beside real `matchHistory` rows and a live balance (:272) — a field that always answers zero next to fields that are real.
  9. **`getMoodState` IS A THRESHOLD TABLE OVER `Reputation`.** `HandleGetMoods` maps `c.Reputation` to five labels and averages it as "mood" (:560-586) — the panel's "mood" is a rename of reputation, not a measure — and `HandleAdjustMood` then WRITES reputation from a CLIENT-SUPPLIED amount via `AdjustReputation(character, amount)` (:597-604).
 10. **THE FILE KEEPS A DEPENDENCY ALIVE TO SILENCE THE COMPILER:** `var _ = strconv.Itoa` (:636) — `strconv` is imported for nothing.
- **VERDICT:** owner (of a second, overlapping public surface) — its garden/mood doors are the corrected lock shape AND document the ABBA cycle they closed, while the money doors in the same file take real balances and return nothing (a purchase with no goods, a tea with no buff), `wagers/resolve` mints a client-chosen payout, and three UI doors (`card/title/equip`, `justice/missions/accept`, `church/members`) report success without performing the action their names promise.
